- **frontmatter**: An agent is a Markdown file with YAML frontmatter (the contract) and a body (the procedure). The frontmatter declares what the agent is, which model runs it, and which tools it must not call. Get the frontmatter wrong and the agent is silently mis-dispatched or loses its MCP access — the body cannot fix a broken contract.
